# PlayNet

Generated by `nn/build.py`; do not edit.

One transformer block looped `loops` times. Every weight is set by the builder from a stated
meaning; nothing is trained. It picks the play `Search.plays` would pick for a hand of 1-8 cards
with any enhancement, edition and seal and up to 5 jokers (main-phase, per-card, counters, input
factors, Midas Mask, Blueprint, Brainstorm and Baseball Card), no boss and
no Plasma Deck balance, and reports its total and hand type.
Every dim is a pure function of the dims and fetches of the loop before, so after `loops` loops
all of them hold their final value. Attention weights are exactly 0, 1, or 1/8, and every
stage is exact float64 arithmetic on integers and multiples of 2^-10.

- residual width d = 4682, hidden units = 13610, loops = 67
- nonzero weights (matrices and biases) = 48367
- levels accepted: mult whole 1..200, chips whole 0..5000
- hands accepted: the encoder bounds from the cards, jokers and levels alone the mult (level mult plus every add, times every doubling and factor 1.5, then the main-phase jokers), the chips and their product; at most 1073741824, 16383 and 4000000000000, and at most 10 factors 1.5 in all (Polychrome, Steel, Baron, Ancient Joker, Baseball Card) plus the fraction bits of the input factors, each counted once per pass
  a card has at most 7 passes (itself, a Red Seal, retriggers), 3 doublings and 2 factors 1.5 per pass; a held card at most 5 passes and 3 factors 1.5 per pass; at most five cards are played, and Hanging Chad and Photograph act on one card
- jokers accepted: at most 5, none debuffed, every key in the lists below, value a whole number 0..500 (for Ice Cream and Popcorn the number in their text); hands left and discards left whole 0..10; plays of a hand type this run and this round whole 0..100
- input factors: the encoder derives a factor from the joker as the reference does (the value; Obelisk value + 0.2; Ramen from its text), and it counts as no effect when it is 1 or less. A factor is accepted only when 4 times it is a whole number and it is at most 8, so 1.25, 1.5, 2.5 and 8 are in scope and 1.1, 1.2, 8.25 and 9 are not (the hand is declined). Ramen, whose factor is rebuilt by subtracting 0.01 from 2 once per discarded card, is in scope only for the text X2 (no discards) and for a factor of 1 or less; every other Ramen factor is not a multiple of 1/4 and declines the hand. The net multiplies by it with shift-and-add over the 6 bits of 4 times the factor, then divides by 4
- copies: Blueprint copies the slot to its right, Brainstorm the first slot, resolved as `resolve` in Scoring.fs (a chain ends at a joker that is not a copy; a loop, the last slot or a missing target gives nothing); a copy takes the key, value and text of its target and keeps its own edition; Baseball Card multiplies each Uncommon slot by 1.5 per slot that acts as it, after that slot's own effect and before its Polychrome
- Midas Mask turns every scoring face card Gold before scoring: the card loses the effect of its enhancement and counts as a plain card of its suit; the hand type is read before
- computed dims on non-candidate tokens hold values nobody reads

## Joker keys in scope

With a score effect once per hand: `j_stuntman`, `j_sly`, `j_wily`, `j_clever`, `j_devious`, `j_crafty`, `j_joker`, `j_gros_michel`, `j_jolly`, `j_zany`, `j_mad`, `j_crazy`, `j_droll`, `j_half`, `j_cavendish`, `j_duo`, `j_trio`, `j_family`, `j_order`, `j_tribe`, `j_blackboard`, `j_flower_pot`, `j_seeing_double`, `j_acrobat`, `j_card_sharp`, `j_drivers_license`, `j_loyalty_card`, `j_abstract`, `j_erosion`, `j_fortune_teller`, `j_swashbuckler`, `j_bootstraps`, `j_ceremonial`, `j_red_card`, `j_flash`, `j_popcorn`, `j_blue_joker`, `j_stone`, `j_bull`, `j_castle`, `j_wee`, `j_ice_cream`, `j_runner`, `j_square`, `j_trousers`, `j_banner`, `j_mystic_summit`, `j_supernova`, `j_green_joker`, `j_ride_the_bus`

With a score effect per card (Ancient Joker and Idol read their target from the text): `j_greedy_joker`, `j_lusty_joker`, `j_wrathful_joker`, `j_gluttenous_joker`, `j_onyx_agate`, `j_fibonacci`, `j_smiley`, `j_even_steven`, `j_scholar`, `j_walkie_talkie`, `j_scary_face`, `j_odd_todd`, `j_arrowhead`, `j_triboulet`, `j_photograph`, `j_idol`, `j_ancient`, `j_hanging_chad`, `j_sock_and_buskin`, `j_hack`, `j_selzer`, `j_dusk`, `j_baron`, `j_mime`, `j_raised_fist`, `j_shoot_the_moon`

Multiplying mult by an input factor: `j_stencil`, `j_steel_joker`, `j_throwback`, `j_constellation`, `j_madness`, `j_hologram`, `j_campfire`, `j_glass`, `j_lucky_cat`, `j_hit_the_road`, `j_yorick`, `j_caino`, `j_ramen`, `j_obelisk`

Copies and the rest: `j_blueprint`, `j_brainstorm`, `j_baseball`, `j_midas_mask`

No score effect, they only occupy a slot: `j_misprint`, `j_superposition`, `j_seance`, `j_vagabond`, `j_matador`, `j_credit_card`, `j_marble`, `j_chaos`, `j_delayed_grat`, `j_egg`, `j_burglar`, `j_faceless`, `j_riff_raff`, `j_cloud_9`, `j_rocket`, `j_luchador`, `j_gift`, `j_turtle_bean`, `j_to_the_moon`, `j_hallucination`, `j_juggler`, `j_drunkard`, `j_golden`, `j_diet_cola`, `j_trading`, `j_mr_bones`, `j_troubadour`, `j_certificate`, `j_ring_master`, `j_merry_andy`, `j_invisible`, `j_satellite`, `j_cartomancer`, `j_astronomer`, `j_burnt`, `j_chicot`, `j_perkeo`, `j_mail`, `j_oops`, `j_8_ball`, `j_business`, `j_ticket`, `j_rough_gem`, `j_reserved_parking`, `j_bloodstone`, `j_hiker`, `j_space`, `j_dna`, `j_todo_list`, `j_sixth_sense`

## Stages

| Stage | Meaning |
|---|---|
| in | input features, set by the encoder |
| card | per-card features: effective rank, suit matches, rank sets, chip value parts |
| fetch | attention heads P1-P5 fetch the features of the candidate's k-th card, H0-H7 those of hand slot s, T averages non-black cards |
| copies | which slots hold a joker that is not a copy, which are Uncommon, and, by rounds, which slot each slot acts as after its Blueprint and Brainstorm copies |
| effective | per slot: the key, value, factor, text bits of the joker it acts as; whether a Midas Mask is held; how many slots act as Baseball Card |
| midas | per scoring card: the chips, enhancements and suit matches it has once a Midas Mask has turned face cards Gold |
| pair | rank equalities, rank gaps, suit counts |
| shape | rank structure of the played cards, flush, straight, scoring-card hints |
| type | one-hot hand type |
| score | which played cards score, level chips and mult |
| situation | no hands or discards left, a round play of the hand type, a joker value of 16 or more, exactly four cards played, another hand type played as often as this one (Obelisk) |
| contained | the hand types the played cards contain, as Hands.containedIn |
| suits | suits the scoring cards cover (Flower Pot, Seeing Double), held cards all black |
| jokers | per joker slot: chips added, mult added, whether it multiplies by 2, 3 or 4 |
| factors | per joker slot: 4 times its factor and its bits, the Baseball Card and Polychrome factors 1.5 it meets |
| order | the first scoring card and the first scoring face card |
| scored | per joker slot and scoring card: target suit and rank matches, the mult and chips it adds, whether it doubles or multiplies by 1.5 |
| passes | per scoring card: passes (itself, Red Seal, retriggers), the affine map one pass applies to mult, one-hots of its shape |
| playedfold | mult after each scoring card: the pass map applied as many times as the card has passes, in played order |
| held | per hand slot: whether it is held, Raised Fist's target, the affine map one held pass applies, its pass count |
| heldfold | mult after each held card in hand order, the held pass map applied as many times as it has passes |
| chips | chips of the scoring cards over all their passes, total chips, chips split into base-128 digits |
| jokerfold | mult folded slot by slot: Holo and its own add, then its factor by shift-and-add, then its Baseball Card factors and Polychrome |
| product | chips times mult, one gated copy of mult per chips digit step |
| floor | floor of the product by base-256 digit extraction, then the total |
| select | key = 256 * total - idx; attention B reads the winner; selector copies it |

## Precision bounds

Worst case under the limits above; each is checked by the builder to fit exactly in float64
(peak times 2^bits is at most 2^52).

| Quantity | Peak | Fraction bits |
|---|---|---|
| mult after every fold stage and joker slot | 1073741824 | 10 |
| gated pre-activation in a played or held fold stage | 2147483650 | 10 |
| mult one card pass adds, with its factors | 972 | 2 |
| gated pre-activation in a card pass step | 1946 | 3 |
| mult one held pass adds, with its factors | 372 | 3 |
| gated pre-activation in a held pass step | 746 | 4 |
| gated pre-activation in the shift-and-add stage of a joker factor | 68719476736 | 10 |
| gated pre-activation in the Baseball Card and Polychrome stage | 16307453954 | 15 |
| chips | 16383 | 0 |
| gated pre-activation of one pass of chips | 702 | 0 |
| gated pre-activation of a joker add (value, banner, supernova) | 1040 | 0 |
| chips times mult | 4000000000000 | 10 |
| gated pre-activation in the product stage | 274877906946 | 10 |
| floor step pre-activation, in units of 2^-10 | 4096000000000000 | 0 |
| chips digit step times its weight | 2097152 | 0 |
| floor digit times its weight | 281474976710656 | 0 |
| selection key | 2048000000000000 | 0 |
